MINISTRY OF PERSONNEL , PUBLIC GRIEVANCES & 

PENSIONS 
(Department of Personnel & Training ) 

New Delhi, the 18th September , 1990 
S . O . 2582. - In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 read with clause ( 5 ) of article 148 of 
the Constituion , and after consultation with the Comptroller 
and Auditor General of India in relation to persone serving 
in the Indian Audit and Accounts Department, the President 
hereby makes the following ruels further to amend the Cen 
tral Civil Services ( Conduct) Rules, 1964, namely: 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Contral Civil Services 
( Conduct) Amendment Rules , 1990 . 

___ ( 2 ) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette . 

2. In the Cential Civil Services ( Conduct) Rules, 1964, for 
Rule 13 of the following Rule shall be substituted, namely :---- 

" 13. GIFTS ( 1 ) Save as provided in these Rules , no Gov 
ernment servant shall accept, or permit any member of his 
family or (any other person acting on his behalf ) to accept, 
any gift . 

EXPLANATION , — The expression “ gift” shall include frec 
transport , boarding lodging or other service or any other 
pecuniary advantage provided by any person other than a 
near relative or personal friend having no official dealings 
with the Govt , servant. 

NOTE ( 1 )- - A casual meal, lift or other social hospitality 
shall not be deemed to be a gift. 

NOTE ( 2 ) -- A Government servant shall avoid accepting 
lavish hospitality or frequent hospitality from any individual, 
industrial or commercial firms, organisations, etc. having offl 
cial dealing with him . 

( 2 ) On occasions, such as wedding anniversaries, func 
rals or religious functions, when the making of a gift is in 
conformity with the prevailing religious or social practice , a 
Govt, servant muy accept gifts from his near relatives but 
shall make report to the Government if the value of any gift 
exceeds: 
( i) Rs. 1000 in the case of a Government servant holding 

any Group A or Group B post; 
( ii) Rs. 500 in the case of a Government servant holding 

any Group C post ; and 
( iii ) Rs. 200 in the case of a Government servant holding 

any Group D post . 

( 3) On such occasions as are specified in sub-rule ( 2 ), 
a Government servant may accept gifts from his personal

tion 4 of the Life Insurance Corporation Act, 1956 (31 of 
1956 ), the Central Government hereby appoints Shri M . G . 
Diwan , Managing Director as Chairman of the Life Ingurance 
Corporation of India with effect from 9th October, 1990 to

( 1 ) उक्त सरकारी कंपनी, उक्त अधिनियम के उपबन्धों के अधीन

पूर्ति करेगी ।

का , प्रा . -- - 2595 - - ~ - जयकि केन्द्र सरकार यह अनुभव करती है कि सार्वजनिक हित में यह आवश्यक है कि पेट्रोलियम पदार्थ 
एवं प्राकृतिक गैस लाने के लिए टाटीपाका - काकीना पाइप लाइन परियोजना के अन्तर्गत पाइप लाइन गैम प्रथारिटी आफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड द्वारा बिछाया 


और यह भी अनुभव करती है कि उस कार्य के लिए इसके साथ संलग्न विपरणी में निर्धारित भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण करना आवश्यक

प्रत : पेट्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार प्रहण ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1982 का 50 ) के खण्ड 3 के उपबन ( 1 ) 
धारा प्रवल शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतवारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार प्रहण करने की मंशा की घोषणा करती है । 

बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी रुचि रखने वाला कोई भी व्यक्तिा अधिमूचमा की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर भूमिगत पाइप लाइन बिछाने के

गैस लाने के लिये टाटीपाका-मामीमाग पाइप लाईन परियोजना के अन्तर्गत पाइप लाईम गम प्रथारिटी माफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड द्वारा बिछाया पामा है ।

का . मा . 2600. — जबकि केन्द्र सरकार यह अनुभव करती है कि सार्वजनिक हित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि पेट्रोलियम पदार्थ एवं प्राकृतिक 
गंग लाने के लिए टाटीपाका-काकीनाग पाइप लाइन परियोजना के अन्तर्गत पाइप लाइन गैस प्रथारिटी आफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड द्वारा बिछाया जाना है । 

मार यह भी अनुभव करती है कि उस कार्य के लिए इसके साथ संलग्न विवरणी में निर्धारित भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण करना प्रावश्यक है । 

प्रतः पट्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाइप लाइम ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) के खण्ड 3 के उपाय ( 1 ) 
धारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतद्द्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार प्रहण करने की मंशा की घोषणा करती है । 

बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी चि रखने वाला कोई भी व्यक्ति अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर भूमिगत पाइप लाइन बिछाने के विरोध 
में अपनी भापति सक्षम प्राधिकारी, गैस प्रथारिटी माफ इण्डिया लिमि . के . जी , बसीन प्रोजक्ट, 12/ 76 प्रकाश नगर , राममंडी- 53310 3, प्रान्न प्रदेश में दर्ज 
करा सकता है । 


मौर ऐसी पापति बज कराते समय किसी भी व्यक्ति को यह विशेष रूप से निर्दिष्ट करना होगा कि वह व्याक्तिगत रूप से अथवा विधि व्यवमायक 
के माध्यम से अपना मत प्रस्तुत करना चाहता है ।

के लिए टाटीपाका-काकीनाडा पाइप लाइन परियोजना के अन्तर्गत पाइप लाइन गैस अथारिटी प्राफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड द्वारा बिछाया जाना है । 

मार यह भी अनुभव करती है कि उस कार्य के लिए इसके साथ संलग्न विवरणी में निर्धारित भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण करना प्रावण्या 


प्रतः पेट्रोलियम एवं निज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण ) अधिनियम , 1982 ( 1962 का 50 ) खण्ड में उपखण्ड ( 1 ) 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतद्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण करने की मंशा की घोषणा करती है । 

बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी रुचि रखने वाला कोई भी व्यक्ति मधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर भूमिगत पाइप लाइन बिछाने के विरोध 
में अपनी प्राप्ति सक्षम प्राधिमारी गैस प्रथारिटी ऑफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड के . जी . बसीन प्रोजेक्ट, 12/ 76 प्रकाश नगर , राजमड़ी- 533103 आन्ध्र प्रदेश में दर्ज 
करा सकता है । 

पोर रोमी यापति दर्ज कराते ममय किमी भी व्यक्ति को यह विशेष रूप में निविष्ट करना होगा कि वह व्यक्तिका रूप से अथवा विधि 
माध्यम में प्राना मत प्रस्तुत करना चाहता है ।

मा . आ . 2604:- - जबकि केन्द्र सरकार यह अनुभव करती है कि मार्वजनिक हित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि पेट्रोलियम पदार्थ एवं प्राकृतिक गैग लाने 
के लिए दाटीपाका याकीनाला पाइप लाइन परिगोजना के अन्तर्गत पाइप लाइन गैस अथारिटी प्राफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड द्वारा बिछाया जाना है । 

मौर यह भी अनुभव करती है कि उस कार्य के लिए इसके स. थ संलग्न विवरणी में निर्धारित भूमि पर प्रमोना का अधिकार ग्रहण करना प्राययक 


प्रतः पेट्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण ) अधिनियम, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) के खण्ड के उपखण्ड ( 1 ) 
वार। प्रवन मितमों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतद्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण करने की मंशा की घोषणा करती है । 

बणत कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी रूचि रखने वाला कोई भी व्यक्ति अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर भूमिगत पाइप लाइन बिष्नान के विरोध 
में प्रपनी प्रापप्ति मक्षम प्राधिकारी गम प्रभारिटी ग्राफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड के . जी . बमीन प्रोजेक्ट, 12/ 76 प्रकाश नगर, राजमंडी - 535 1102, प्रान्प्राण में 
घर्ज करा सकता है । 

प्रौर ऐसी प्रापत्ति दर्ज कराते समय किमी भी व्यक्ति को ग्रह विशेष रुप से निर्दिष्ट करना होगा कि वह व्यक्तिगत रुप में प्रथका विधि व्यवसाय के 
माध्यम से अपना मत प्रम्सुन करना चाहता है ।

का , प्रा . 3606: — यन : केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत 
होता है कि लोकहित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि गजरात राज्य में ईपी 
एस से अंकलेश्वर मीटी एफ तक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये 
पाइपलाइन तेल तथा प्राकृतिक गैस आयोग द्वाग बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

मौर, प्रतः, यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयो 
जन के लिए एतदपाबर अनुमूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधि . 
कार अर्जित करना आवश्यक है । 

प्रतः, मन , पेट्रोलियम पीर खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग 
के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम, 1962, ( 1962 का 50) की 
घारा की उपधाग द्वारा प्रवन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय 
सरकार ने उममें उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपना प्राणय 
एतद्वारा घोषित किया है । 

मगर्ने कि उक्त भूमि में हितपर कोई व्यक्ति , उस भूमि के नीचे 
पाइप लाइन बिछाने के लिए प्राक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी, तेल तथा प्राकृतिक 
गंम प्रायोग, निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग, मकरपुरा गेड, बड़ौदा -- 9 
को इम अधिसूचना की तारीख मे 21 दिनों के भीतर कर मझेगा । 

और ऐसा प्राभेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिर्दिष्टतः यह भी 
फथन करेगा कि क्या यह वह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिग 
रूप से हो या फिसी विधि व्यवसायी की मार्फत ।

का . मा . 160 9. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
लोकहित में यह पावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में जी एन जी एन से 
ई पी एस तक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइपलाइन तेल तथा 
प्राकृतिक गैस मायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और प्रतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतद्गायद अनुसूची म णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार 
पोजत करना भावश्यक है । 

प्रतः प्रब पद्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का भने ) माधनियम, 1982 ( 1962 का 50 ) को धारा 3 
की उपधारा द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयाग करत हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार ने 
उसम उपवाग का प्रधिकार प्राजत करने का अपना प्राशय एतद्वारा 
भोषित किया है । 

बशर्ते कि उपत भूमि में तिबर कोई व्यक्ति , उस भूमि के नीचे 
पारप लाइन बिछाने के लिए भाक्षप सक्षम प्राधिकारो, तेल तथा प्राकृतिक 
गैस पायाग, निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग, मकरपुरा रोग, बडौवा- 9 को 
इस माधसूचना को तारोख से 21 दिनों के भातर कर सकेगा । 

मोर एसा प्राक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति मिमिनिष्टतः यह भी कथन 
करेगा कि क्या यह वह चाहता है कि उसको सुनवाई व्यक्तिगत रूप स 
हो या किसो विधि व्यबसाया की मार्फत । 


का . प्रा . 2010.- - यत: झेन्द्रीय सरार को यह प्रतीत हो। है 
कि लोकहित में यह मावश्यक है कि गजरात राज्य में पावरा ई पी एस 
से के सी एफ तक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइपलाइन तेल तथा 
प्राकृतिक गैस प्रयोग द्वार बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और प्रतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइन। को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतदपाबद्ध अनुमूची में गणित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार 
पजित करना भावश्यक है । 

प्रतः अब पेट्रोलियम मौर बनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 , ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 
3 को उपधारा द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तिया का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
ने उसमें उपयोग का अधिकार भषित करने का प्रपना प्राशय एतद्वारा 
घोषित किया है । 
___ यशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में हितबब कोई व्यक्ति, उस भूमि के नीचे

इस मधिसूचमा की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा ।

करेगा कि क्या यह वह चाहता है कि उसको सुनबाई व्यक्तिगत रूप से हो 
या किसी विधि व्यवसायी मी मार्फत ।

का . प्रा . : G1 : यतः केन्द्रीय मरकार की यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
लोकहित में यह मावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में जी . एन . ए . एफ 
से एन जी 4 मक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाइपलाइन सेल तथा 
प्राकृतिक गम आयोग द्वारा बिठाई जानी चाहिये । 

और अनः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को बिछाने 
लिये एतदपाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजिन 
करना प्रविश्यक है । 

अतः अब पद्रोलियम पऔर खनिज पाइपलाइन ( मि मे उपयोग 
मधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50) की धाग 3 
की उपधाग द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार ने 
उममें उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का प्रपना प्राणय एतददाग 
घोपित किया है । 

मशर्ते कि उक्त भमि में हितबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति, उम भमि के नी 
पाइप लाइन बिछाने के लिये प्राक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी , नेल तथा प्राकृतिक 
गम आयोग , निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग, मफम्परा , रोड, बड़ौदा 
को इस अधिसूचमा की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर मकेगा ।

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 6, 1990 / ASVINA 14, 1912 


[ PART II --- SEC . 3( ii ) ] 


प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . मा , स : 1360 सारीख 
14- 4- 88 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरफार मे उस अधिसूधमा से मलग्न अनुसूची से 
विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाईनों को बिछाने 
के लिये अर्जित करने का अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

भौर यतः मक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

पार भागे, यत: केन्द्रीय मरफार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने 
के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

प्रथ, प्रप्तः उक्स अधिनियम की धारा 6 को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केंद्रीय सरकार एसद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस प्रधिसूचमा में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियो 
में उपयोग का अधिकार पाहपलाईन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये एसद् 
द्वारा पर्जित किया जाता है । 


And, further , in exerciso of power conferred by sub -section 
( 4 ) of the section, the Contral Government directs that the 
right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting in 
Central Government vests on this date of tho publication of 
this declaration in the Oil and Natural Gas Commission 
free from encumbrances.

प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों में

और प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग में , सभी बाधामों से मक्त कप में , 
घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

का . प्रा . - 3814 यातः केन्द्रीय मरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
लोकहित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में पावरा 9 से ई पी एस 
पादग तक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाइपलाईम तेल तथा 
प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग माग बिछाई जानी चाहिये । 

और अनः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाईनों को बिछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिये पनपाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का 
भधिकार अभित करना आवश्यक है । 

प्रतः प्रम पैट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50) की धारा 3 
की उपधारा द्वारा प्रदन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
ने उममें उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपना प्राशय एतद 
द्वारा घोषित किया है ।

पारुप लाईन बिछाने के लिये प्राक्षेप मक्षम प्राधिकारी , नेल नथा 
प्राकृतिक गैम आयोग, निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग , मकरपुरा रोड,

मप मे हो या किसी विधि व्यवसायी की मार्फत ।

इस अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा ।

प्रयोजन के लिए एतपाबद्ध अनुमचं में गिन भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार

पौर यतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिये एतपाबर अनुसूषो में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार 
अर्जित करना मावश्यक है । 

प्रत: अब पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का पर्जन ) अधिनियम 1962 (1982 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा द्वारा ( 1 ) प्रवत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार

घोषित किया है । 


[ मं . ओ - 11027/ 65 / 00 - ओएनजीसी UI ] 


S .O . 2630 .- -Whereas it appears to the Central Gov 
crament that it is necessary in the public interest that 
for the transport of petroleum from DHN to KGGS in 
Gujarat State pipeline should be laid by the Oil & Natural 
Gas Commission . 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying 
such pipeline, it is necessary to acquire that right of usor 
in the land described in the schedule annexed hereto ; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub -section ( 1 ) of the Section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in the 
land ) Act, 1962 ( 50 of 1962) , the Central Government 
hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user 
therein , 

Provided that any person interested in the said land may, 
within 21 days from the date of this notification , object 
to the laying of the pipeline under the land to the Competent 
Authority , Oil & Natural Gas Commission, Construction & 
Maintenance Division , Makarpura Road ; Vadodara - 390009. 


बशर्ते कि उक्त भमि में हितमत कोई व्यक्ति , उस भूमि के नीचे पाइप 
लाइन बिछाने के लिए ग्राभेप, सक्षम प्राधिकारी, तेल तथा प्राकृतिक गैस 
आयोग निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग, मकरपुरा रोड, बड़ौदा- 9 को इस 
अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 

और ऐसा आक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिविष्टसः यह भी कथन 
करेगा कि क्या यह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिगत रूप से हो या 
किम विधि व्यवसायी के मार्फत ।

फा . मा . 2633 . -- यप्तः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
लोकहित में यह आवश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में जी . एन . ए . वाय 
( षधकपी ) से नाडा-1 हीडर तक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाइप 
लाइन सेल तथा प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और अतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतवपायव मनुसूधि में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार 
मर्जित करना आवश्यक है । 

प्रतः अब पेट्रोलियम पीर खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का मर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962, ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 
की उपधाराबारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार ने 
उसमें उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपना प्राशय एतद्वारा 
घोषित किया है । 

या कि उक्त भूमि में हितबर कोई व्यक्ति उस भूमि के नीचे 
पाइप लाइन बिछाने के लिए प्राक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी, तेल तथा प्राकृतिक 
गस मायोग, निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग, मकरपुरा रोड, बड़ौवा- 9 को 
इस मधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 विनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 

और ऐसा प्राक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिविष्टसः यह भी कथन 
करेगा कि क्या यह वह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिगत रूप से 
हो या किसी विधि व्यवसायी की मार्फत ।

S .O . 2635 .-- Whereas it appears to the Central Gov . 
ernment that it is necessary in the public interest that for 
the transport of petroleum from GNEF to GNBI Header 
in Gujarat State pipeling should be laid by the Oil & 
Natural Gas Commission . 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying 
such pipeline, it is necessary to acquire that right of user 
In the land described in the schedule annexed hereto ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub- section ( 1) of the Section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipelines ( Acquisition of Right of User in the 
land ) Act , 1962 (50 of 1962 ), the Central Government 
hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user 
therein ; 

Provided that any person interested in the said land may , 
within 21 days from the date of this notification , object 
to the laying of the pipeline under the land to the Competent 
Authority , Oil & Natural Gas Commission , Construction & 
Maintenance Division, Makarpura Road ; Vadodara- 390009 . 

And every person making such an objection shall also 
state specifically whether he wishes to be hear in person 
or by legal Practitioner .

से टी बिन्दु सफ पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिये पाईपलाईन तेल तथा 
प्राकृतिक गैस पायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए ।

के लिए एतदपाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भमि में उपयोग का अधिकार 
अजित करना प्रावश्यक है ।

उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपना प्राणय एतद्वारा घोषित 
किया है ।

पायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए ।

अधिकार फा अर्मन ) अधिनियम , 1982 ( 1982 का 50 ) की धारा 3

के लिए एतवपापय अनुसूपि में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित

गायोग, निर्माण और देवनाल प्रमाग, मकरयुग रोड, बड़ोदा - को इस

even on the basis of injunction order the previous suspension 
order would not revive after vacation of the injunction 
order. The concerned workman has been discriminated

been revised although the pay of other employees and 
suspended workman have been revised with effect from 1- 8 -83

cerned workman by conducting enguiry on dropped charges

sion hevond 6 months hut in the case of the concerned work .

Point No . 2 
In view of my finding on point No . 1 , I further hold that 
the action of the management in denying full wages and 
other benefits to the concerned worknian even after the 
revolution of th : two suspension orderá is ro legal 
and justified . It has been submilied on behalf of the 
workmen that even his pay has noi been revised by the 
management with effect from 1 - 2 - 3 although all the staff 
of FCI have drawn their arrears but the pay scale of the 
concerned workman has not been revised. The management 
suited that as the matter regarding his suspension and 
punishment in the CBI case is pending consideration , the 
salary oi tho concerned workman has not been revised under 
the revised pay scale of the FCI. There is no ground for 
not revising the pay scale of the workman as he was put on 
suspension . The management was boi:nd to fix his pay in the 
revised scale from the date it came into force in respect of 
the other workman of FCI. . The management has shown no 
rule or order to show that pay of an employee of FCT cannot 
be revised during the period of his suspension . I hold there 
fore that the management must revise the pay scale of the 
concerned workman from the date other workman of the 
FCT have been allowed revised pay scales. 


I hold that the action of the management in denying 
revised full wages and other benefits to the concerned work 
man even after revokation of the suspension order and 
resumption of his duties is no : legal and justified .

The verification Ext. W - 16 declares that the order of sus

In view of the discussion made above I hold that the 
charges relating to the enquiry procepding against the 
concerned workman has not been dropped by the management 
in a thority and as such it appers that the action of the 
management in conducting anguiry into the charges against 
the concerned workman is legal and justified . 

In the result, I ho d that the action of the management 
of FCI, Patna is not justifieri in placing the concerned

Point No . 3 


The case of the concerned workman is that the charges 
against him have heen dropped by the inanagement and as

Cenrai vovt. vide Gazette notification No. L - 12012 | 3251 
86 - 0 .11( A ) dated 3rd December 1987 issued U / S 10 ( 1 )( d ) 
of industrial Disputes Act 1947 referred the following dis 
Pits to this Trivunal for decision on a dispute raised by 
Shri Kasturi Lal : 


“ Wiether tho action of the management of State Bank 

of Patiala , is justified in declaring the services of 
Shri Kasturi Lal Cashier -cum -Clerk as abandoned 
because he availed long unauthorised leave ? If not, 
to what relief the workman concerned is entitled ? ” 


2 . Present case was taken up at Lok Adalat today . The 
parties have amicably settled the dispute . Shri P . P . Tandon 
Industrial Relation Officer appearing on behalf of the 
management has stated that Kasturi Lal has offered re 
employment from the date of his joining latest by 1st 
August 1990 . His salary will be fixed by giving four incre 
ments earned by him during the previous employment from 
1972 to 1976 and in addition he will also be given four 
more increments without any arrears in this regard . He 
has also stated that workman has to forgo his claim for 
back wages and other monetary benefits in this regard and 
also forgo any claim of seniority and continuity of service 
and other service benefits except seniority for the period he 
had already served . 


2 . The instant case was taken up for cross examination 
of the workrran on 10 - 5 . 90, when the workman did not 
turn up . On the said date it was ordered that a notice be 
sent to the workman fixing 28 - 6 - 90 . On 28 - 6 - 90 the work 
man appeared but the cross examination of the workman 
could not over on account of the fact that the notice was 
sent to the Union which raſced the dispute an ! rotto 
the workman whereas it ought to have ben 32 !1! to the 
workman as he him elf aprearing in the case . As ell h one 
more poportunity was given to him for his cross examination 
and 27- 8 - 90 was fixed in the case . From the order sheet 
it appears that he put his initials on the order sheet in token 
of having come to know about the next date , in the 
case . On 27 - 8 - 90 he did not turn up despite having full 
knowledge about the date and purpose of the case . 

3 . Thus from the above , it appears that neither the Union 
nor the workman himself is interested in prosecuting the 
case otherwise he should have presented before the Tribunal 
for his cross examination . As such a no claim award is 
given in the case against the workman /Union . 
4 . Reference is answered accordingly . 

ARJAN DEV , Presiding Officer 

[No. L - 12012 /97 188- D .I][(A ) ] 


Kasturi Lal workman has accepted this offer of re- employ 
ment offered by the management on the terms and condi 
tions stated above . 


3 . In view of this statement that parties have settled the 
dispute and the workman has approved the offer made by 
the management thus a no dispute award is returned .

The Central Gover ninent in the Ministry of Ladoui vido 
its Order No , L - 12012 / 109 / 84 -DIIA dated 6 - 12 - 84 has re 
ferred the following industrial dispute to this Tribunal for 
adjudication : 
" Whether the action of the management of Allahabad 

Bank , in terminating the services of Shri kanı Babu 
Sharma, sub- staff from October , 1979 while retain 
ing his juniors in the service and not considering 
him for further employment when subsequently re 
cruiting fresh hands, is justified ? If not, to what 
relief is the worknian conccinesl entitled ? 


" That the workman being aggrieved preferred depart 

mental appeal when again the Appellate Authority 
finding no reason to interfere with the decision of 
the Disciplinary Authority confirmed the punish 
ment of Dismissal imposed on the workman by 
the Disciplinary Authority ." 


4 . I find that the misconduct committed by the gald work 
man is quite serious and he deserved the punishment of dis 
missal from service of the Bank , Therefore , the action taken 
by the Bank management is quite just and proper . 


2 . The brief facts of this casc ure that Shri Ram Bahu 
Sharma, the worknian concerned initially joinod Allahabad 
Bank as a temporary peon in June 1976 at Delhi Gula 
Ghaziabad Branch , He was further given tempo 
rary employment at the same branch and at two other 
branches at Ghaziabad from time to time. No cmployment 
was given to the workman aller October , 1979. According 
to the workman he had worked for 303 days during the 
year 1976 to 1979 though the actual days of working comes 
to 293 days. The services of the workman were iermipated 
in October , 1979 while officials junior to him were retained , 
Since the workman had completed more than 240 days so 
he was entitled to regular cmployment as was dony in the 
case of other employees who had completed the same period , 


5. The following Award is therefore passed , 


AWARD 


The action of the management of Dona Bank in dismissing 
from service Shri B . V . Kelanbekar is justified . 

The parties to bear their own costs of this Reference. 


3 . The management on the other hand alleged lisat there 
was inordinate delay in moving the matter on behalf of the 
workman . However , the main point lirged by thọ manage 
ment way that the workman had not worked for 240 days 
during any 12 calenılar months. Since this requirement of 
law was not fulfilled so he was not entitled to any rogular 
employment.

visor and was fixed in Techrical and Supervisory Gracle D 
taking into consideration his the then existing condition of 
service at the time of his transfer from Bhagabund Colliery . 
Tlio Coke Bhutta of Laikdih Deep Colliery was closed for 
many years and reopened in December, 1985 . During the 
above period the concerned workman was put on alternative 
job because of closure of Coke Bhatta , A9 6oon as Coke 

Bhat was reopened in December , 1985 he was posted hack 
as Bhalla Supervisor to look after the Hard Coke Oven . 
His case was considered for pronotion in 1986 and he was 
promoted from Iechnical und Supelvisory Grade D to Grade 
" C" with effect fion 22 - 5 - 1986 . As he liad not yet coni 
pleted minimum three yers experience in Grade C , his 
Case for monotion from Gradc C to Grado B could not 
be considered . Supervisory personnels are placed in non 
Technical Clerical Grade II to Special Grade or Technical 
Grade D to Technical Grade A depending upon the nature 
and qualily of supervision , extent of control exercise by a 
supervisor over the workmen and extent of responsibility 
shouldered by him . There exists cadre scheme for different

trades, but there exists no cadre scheme for Bhatta Supervi 
sor and they are promoted according to individual merits. 
Bhatta Supervisors remain a clerical grade so long as he 
supervises the workmen employed in the Coke Oven Plant 
and entire technical job is supervived and controlled by Assit. 
Manager or Foreman - in -charge. After he acquires skill and 
technical knowledge , he is considered for his conversion to 
Technical and Supervisory Grade D or C as the case may 
be. In the course of years he is prorioted from Grade I 
to Grade C and from Grade "Cº to Grade B on the 
basis of his merit. Thus, a Bhatta Supervisor may be placed 
in any grade from Grade D to Grade B or from Grade 
III to Grade 1. The concerned workman has been given 
proper grade appropriate to his merit , and he cannot claim 
Grade B on the basis that his designation is Bhatta Super 
visoi. 


Cross -examined on this point. Besides, the noting sheet of

evidence mentioned above (Ex. W - 2 ) leads me to conclude 
that the concerned workinan was working as Bhatta Super 
visor and placed in Technical Grade D with effect from 
sometimt in 1974 ,

with no less responsibility than the job of Bhatta Supervisor. 
His case is for placement in proper grade and not a case for 
promotion .

The case of the management is that there exists no cadre

8 . According to Shri Ghosh the function of the 
washery is to wash my coal for supply to the Steel 
Plan and that during the process of wa hing the quan 
tity of ash contain in coal is reduced and other quali 
ties of coal as substance of energy are improveci . The 
principal function of the washery as disclosed in 
the evidence of Shri Ghosh is a hard evidence which 
is not disputed . 

Shri Ghosh is the snie witness for the manage 
ment , he has stated in his testimony that by the pro 
cess of washing of coal in the washery effluent , 
consisting of water , fine particles of coal and 
sandy particles, comes out of the washery and that 
the composition of effluent varies from time to time. 
According Shri Ghosh such effluent contains 25 per 
cent of solid and goes down to the effluent pond call 
ed also slurry pond and solid particles of coal settle 
down in the pond and washery portion is recvled 
back to the washery and this solid portion which 
settles down in the pond is excavated . In cross 
examination he has admitted that wa hery s siurry 
and washerv s effluent are inter - changeal,le term and 
admitted that the management has not filed any docti 
ment to establish the fact that total percentage of 
the solid in the slurry is about 25 per ceni. As a 
matter of fact the managernent has not disclosed the 
total percentage of solid in the slurry in its writren 
statement- cum -rejoinder . Thu , the broad fact that 
emerges from the evilence on rrcord is that by the 
process of washing of coal in the washery effort 
comes down which is deposited in slurry rond or 
effluent pond . 

The process of washing of coal in the Coal 
Washery , as the cvidence reveals, is required for 
benefitiation of coal in order to make the same suis 
able for use in the Steel Pani by reducing the ash 
quantity and improving other quantities of coal as 
substance of energy .

8 . It appears that the claim of the concerned work 
man for absorption regularisation in the service of 
Gidi Washery and Ms. C . C . Ltd . is principally based 
on this agreement. The case of the management is 
that the concerned workman had never worked as 
ninnt cleaning mazdoors and that they are attempting

In the register of the contractor - M /s. Sengupta & 
Sons by somemanipulation and collusion between the

and collusion with the concerned workmen . The 
sponsoring union has hotly disputed such manipula 
tion and collusion . At the time of hearing the man 
agement has tried to fish out evidence to suggest by 
examining MW - 3 N . P . Singh who was posted in 
Argada Arca of Ms. C . C . Ltd . as Personnel Man

management after the sponsoring union has closed its

these circumstances, Shri D . Mukherjee , authorised 
representative of the sponsoring union , has criticised 
the course adopted by the management in examining 
MW - 3 after close of the evidence of the sponsoring 
mion by Submitting that the management has tried 
hy having recourse to this method , to fill in the lacuna 
hy ramification and other means. Indeed , the testi 
mony of Shri Singh is replete with hard facts not dis 
closed in the pleading of the management. Anyway , 
Shri Singh has stated that Mr. N . P . Srivastava , Lab 
our Officer, inserted the names of the concerned 
workmen in the register and that the management 
decided to take disciplinary action against him and 
when he got scent that things brewing hot for him he 
left Mls , C . C . Itd . This fact has not been pleaded 


911 the pleading ner hes the management submitted 
any document in support of this fact. In CTOSS -cxami 
nation this witness has admitted that Sri Srivastava 
resigned from service and that when a person resigns 
from service , he will not be released until and unless 
his resignation is accepted . He has further admitted 
that the resignation letter of Shri Srivastava and ac 
ceptance of his resignation must be lying with the 
management. The big question remains as to how the 
management accepted his resignation when he alle 
gedly played dirty trick with it. There is 10 answer 
to this question . 
In the circumstances , I have reason to beelieve that 
the managerjent has made Sri Srivastava a scape 
goat in order to lend credence to its case that the 
names of the concerned workinen were inserted in 
the register of the contractor by manipulation und 
collusion between interested persons and the concer 
ned worktuen , Then again , it renrains unexplained as 
to why Sri Srivastava became interested in the employ

of Central Coalfieds Ltd . in not alsorbing depart 
mentalising S Shri (i) Dhaneshwar - l, (2 ) Duria

settlement ( Ext. M - 2 ) is not justified . The manage 
ment is directed to absorb !departmentalise the con 
cerned workmen as Category - ] Mazdocrs with effect 
from 18- 2 - 1983 and to pay them 50 per cert of back 
wages from that date till the date of their joining 
duties. The concerned workmen are directed to 
report for duties within one month from the date of 
publication of this award . 
In the circumstances of the case , I award no cost .

had that been so , the concerned workman should 
have been arraigned on charge for having nade less 
payment to 14 workmen , It appears that Sri B . P . 
Singh , Sri Rariran Singh and Sri T , K , Diar stated 
in writing thot the concerned workman agreed to 
make payment of balance amount to the workers 
concerned within two days from the date when the 
occurrence was rccrted on 22- 6 - 83 . The management 
produced the statement of these three witnesses in 
the domestic coquiry . But none of these witnesses 
wers produced before the Enguiry Officer for their 
cross - examination by the concerned workman . Then 
again , all these seven concerned workmen who alleged 
ly received less payment than what were due to them 
submitted an application on 22 - 5 -83, i.e . when the

also produced this document before the Enquiry 
Officer. But the Enquiry Officer did not consider this

statement of witnesses and the report of the Adminis 
trative Officer that the concerned workman had 
Geducted the money illegally from the workman , the 
Enquiry Officer found the concerned worknian guilty 
of the charge levelled against him . I have already 
pointed out that the opinion of Sri Sinha , the Ad 
ministartive Officer that the concerned workman as 
admitted to have made less payments is unwarranted . 
Besides , the cninion of the Administrative Officer 
cannot be considered as decisive factor for holding 
the concerned workmar guilty of the charge of 
misconduct. 

11. It appears that Madan Mohli, Kalipada Kalindi, 
Nibaran Bouri , Sukhee Bibi and Budhu Munda were

could not also state the amount that were dut to him

12 . Regard being had to the discrepancy ir to

general law and ordur situation nor the vulu VI 
Inemor or his workmen for their well regulated Chile 
turit advancernent. li the power to regulate the be 
hlavivur of the workmen outside the duty hours and 
vid willy juder bow wiicrever ( y may be was conferinil 
upou the employer , the contract of service may ha 
ruuuced to contract of slavery . The employer is on 
titlecl io prescribe conditons of service more or less 
specuzing the acts of misconduct to be enforced within 
in the premises where the workinen gather together 
for rendering service . The ciaployer has both power 
and jurisdict101] to regulate the behaviour ol 1110 
workach within the premises of the establishment 
or for peacefully carrying the industrial activities in 
the vicinity of the establishment. Having the above 
principle in view it will appear that the concerned 
workman was claiming his right title and possession 
Over the land alleged to have been encroached tiy 
him and which is being claim . d as the land belongin 
to the management. There is absolutely s authentic 
evidence to show that the land alleged to have been 
encroached and belonging to the monagement was in 
tact the land of the management. Th : 2 -1 1111t the 
concerned workman had at the very outset whiíu ro

agement had any doubt he could get the land measure

tion ! within the area of the land of the manage 
ment. I have already stated above that there is 
no evidence to show that the alleged encroached land 
actually belonged to the inanagement of Tisco and as 
such it was too niuch for the management to ask the 
concerned workman to Cove the construction being 
claimed hy the concerned workman . The dispute 
over the land was out and out a Civil dispute reunir 
ing a decision from the competent court so that the 
right title and possession over the disputed land couiu 
be vecided . 

If the land being claimed by the management ac 
tually belonged to the concerned workmani and he had 
his construction on it since long, it cannot be said that 
the concerned workman had disobeyed any lawful 
Order of the managenient s officer and hence the con 
cerned workman was within his right to refuse to re 
move the encroachunent unless it was measured and 
found that there was any encroachment by the con 
cerned workman oyer. the land of the management. If 
in the above background the concerned workman told 
the Security Inspector to do anything he liked to do 
and for which he woull feel the consequences, there
